Theorem 230.1 (Factorization Result)

Statement: The logic variety V defined by Factorization Result constraints admits a stratification into 757 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 873 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: Consider the Factorization Result operator L on the logic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 317 for all k. By the Factorization Result theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 873. QED.
